| bridge-ask-en | en | pass | judge: One decision card names the recipient as terminal-claude, and the message carries the user's ask without numbered sub-questions. ### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos y mantener el historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar tal cual cuándo y cómo se integró el trabajo, porque nunca conviene reescribir historia que otros ya tienen.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的（保证数据不丢、不乱序、按序交付，但有握手和重传开销）；UDP 是不可靠、无连接的（尽快发出去，不保证到达，但开销小、延迟低）。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unpushed branch to tidy it up before you share it or merge it. Merge when the branch is shared or others already build on it, since rebasing rewrites history they may have pulled.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/math.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名或路径里的一段，Bun 会匹配包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test math.test
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test math.test.ts -t "adds numbers"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data files. If the system crashes midway, the database replays that log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.
```

### noticed-how-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你怎么知道我要去 AGI Bar 的？是哪天来着

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 compaction 那个 PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到 review 里提到的锁粒度问题，把锁的范围缩小（只锁真正需要保护的数据，减少持锁时间和互斥范围），改在当前分支上。
- 改完跑相关测试，确认 compaction 的行为没变，结果附在报告里。

完成标准：
- 改动已提交到该 PR 的分支上。
- 报告里写清楚：改了哪几处锁、各自从什么范围缩到什么范围，以及测试输出。

不在范围内：
- 不合并，不把 draft 改成 ready，不推送到远程，不在 PR 上发评论。推送和发评论等用户点头。

地点：原来的仓库和分支，不新开。
```

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.

(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the nightly results so far (nights 2 and 3), and it will add each new night as the benchmark reports.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb 写入基准 / hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1958 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="p">The first run is still to come</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Each night's write speed, p99 latency and compaction stalls will list here.</div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var nights = [];
  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!nights.length) { empty.style.display = ''; return; }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = 'Night ' + n.n;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = n.writes;
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.appendChild(main);
      wrap.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(wrap);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes;
    document.getElementById('p').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  }
  if (window.ducter) {
    ducter.get('nights').then(function (v) { nights = v || []; render(); });
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      ducter.get('nights').then(function (v) { nights = v || []; render(); });
    });
  }
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"n":2,"writes":"181k/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"n":3,"writes":"182k/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

  function save(){ if (window.ducter) ducter.set('items', items); }

  function load(){
    if (!window.ducter) { render(); return; }
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(function(v){
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  $('day').value = '11-23';

  $('save').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
    var day = $('day').value.trim() || '未定';
    items.push({ id: Date.now() + '' + Math.floor(Math.random()*1000), day: day, what: $('what').value.trim(), amount: amount });
    $('what').value = '';
    $('amt').value = '';
    save();
    render();
  });

  $('days').addEventListener('click', function(e){
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
      items = items.filter(function(i){ return String(i.id) !== id; });
      save();
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) row.classList.toggle('open');
  });

  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

function $(i){return document.getElementById(i)}
